# Master feature acceptance matrix

> **Current acceptance status (2026-10-16):** Use the [fresh ECC acceptance ledger](acceptance-ledger-2026-10-16.json). It records **213/384 core requirements locally verified (55.47%)**, with 70 implemented, 56 partial, 41 blocked, and 4 failed. The 30,000-user target is deferred and excluded. The detailed matrix below is the historical 2026-10-13 snapshot; its statuses and test counts are superseded by the fresh ledger.

---

## Historical audit snapshot (2026-10-13)

Date: **2026-10-12–13 (Asia/Manila)** (audit began October 12, finalized October 13). Baseline: `41c0ea1a7937035db6078cbb944f1e94f2fbad63`. Audited HEAD at generation: `d2d09ba9028448d7e2a88320643b1aaafb9004af`. Final tested implementation commit: `ecfe3f42963896cd86a291450fa574a4c26b197f`; report commit is discoverable through Git history.

Environment: Darwin arm64, CPython 3.11.16, isolated synthetic SQLite/TestClient and Chromium; separate Linux/Docker Python 3.12.14 PostgreSQL/Redis/runtime proof receipts. The full current backend run passed **1132**, skipped **18**, failed **0**; exact records are in [backend-final.xml](evidence/master-2026-10-12/backend-final.xml). Final functional+visual browser run: **283 PASS, 35 SKIPPED, 0 FAIL** ([playwright-final-candidate.log](evidence/browser-2026-10-12/playwright-final-candidate.log)). Earlier failures are retained in companion test/remediation reports; browser results below apply only where explicitly attached. Historical reports are context, not new execution.

Control snapshot: **2,130 visible instances / 2,099 focusable controls**, zero enabled-but-unfocusable controls, zero missing accessible names and zero invalid composite widgets, generated 2026-10-12T13:34:04.014Z ([receipt](evidence/CONTROL_INVENTORY_2026-10-12.json)). This fixture-dependent inventory checks entry points/names/focus; it does not accept every control interaction.

Original A–R bullets: **384**, including the ten visual regression bullets in A and nineteen control checklist bullets in C. Five prose clarifications are separately identified and excluded from the denominator. Verified local acceptance: **207/384 = 53.91%**. This is feature acceptance for stated local scope, not production readiness.

Percent rules: full local implementation = VERIFIED COMPLETE + IMPLEMENTED BUT UNVERIFIED; partial/blocker foundations are shown separately as any implementation presence. Presence is not completion, and externally blocked integrations are never live-verified. No product deferral is inferred without an explicit product decision.

Commands/evidence: memory-only openpyxl XLSX and pypdf PDF reader smoke; `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_pdf_report_layout.py` (2 PASS,0 FAIL/SKIP,0.07s); request parsing; `rg --files`, `rg -n` symbol/endpoint/test searches; source/doc reads; parent `pytest` run (JUnit above), security focused boundary run (see MASTER_SECURITY_REVIEW), separate browser execution (see MASTER_UI_UX_REVIEW). Inventory author made no application changes; discovered/reproduced PDF report layout and legacy publication defects, and verified root/security-owned corrections.

A source-only result stays unverified. A passing suite does not accept unasserted features. Host PostgreSQL/Redis/Linux/systemd skips remain in the host receipt; separate native PostgreSQL/Redis/Linux/Docker scopes pass where explicitly attached. Live providers/hotel hardware, clean-host installs and physical mobile acceptance remain blocked. Refer to [gap analysis](MASTER_PRODUCT_GAP_ANALYSIS.md) for risks and prioritized actions.
